the make in Gugan 5's drives (README.md:510-511): "The balls were Dawson 'Internationals', all in excellent condition: independent measurements of their CoR were not made"
whether the makes differ (README.md:516-518): "So the makes differ: the face fits come from a Jaques Eclipse and the targets from Dawson Internationals." Recorded, not corrected; the fit takes the face fits as sourced and moves only its listed candidates.
le Maitre's spread (README.md:522-526): the stop shot's, forward ball over backward ball, not his drive's: "Calculation of the ratio of distance travelled by the forward ball to the backward ball gives a value of 4.2 for Walker, 3.7 for Barlow, 3.2 for old Jaques and 2.7 for new Jaques balls."
  his drive or roll (README.md:528-533) is a weighted-head follow-through stroke, not Gugan 5's pendulum drive; the README copies his printed ratios. Neither set is a target.
  Walker: stop ratio 4.2; drive or roll 8.260 m forward, 3.300 m backward, printed ratio 2.8, his own distances give 2.5
  Barlow: stop ratio 3.7; drive or roll 7.000 m forward, 3.460 m backward, printed ratio 2.0, his own distances give 2.0
  Old Jaques: stop ratio 3.2; drive or roll 7.290 m forward, 3.440 m backward, printed ratio 2.1, his own distances give 2.1
  New Jaques: stop ratio 2.7; drive or roll 6.340 m forward, 3.180 m backward, printed ratio 1.8, his own distances give 2.0
